## Decisions

_(ทางที่เลือกเมื่อ spec ไม่ชัด — วันที่ · หัวข้อใน SPEC · สิ่งที่ตัดสินใจ · เหตุผล)_

| วันที่ | อ้างอิง | ตัดสินใจ | เหตุผล |

| 2026-11-01 | M5 S1 · FR-906 / M5-D5 / R16 (affectedWeeks docstring) | affectedWeeks returns the Mondays of every week that at least one range touches, deduplicated and in ascending order. It does not fill every week from weekStart(min start) to weekStart(max end), and it throws a RangeError when an end date is before its start date. | This gives the same result as the plan for every case in its table. A week that no range touches cannot change capacity, so leaving it out loses no information. It also keeps an update's list at most 2 × 53 weeks, even when the old and new ranges are years apart (R16). Filling the whole span would have no upper bound. (S1) |

| 2026-11-01 | M5 S1 · FR-901 / M5-D11 (S1.2 parser details) | Each line gets one error, the first rule it fails, checked in this order: COLUMNS → BAD_DATE → EMPTY_NAME → NAME_TOO_LONG → DUPLICATE. Errors are sorted by line. `rows` holds only valid lines. fileError EMPTY means there are no data records and no errors. TOO_MANY counts data records, valid or not, above 500. TOO_LARGE returns encoding 'utf-8' without decoding the file. Trailing empty fields are removed only beyond the second field, so '2026-10-23,' gives EMPTY_NAME and not COLUMNS. Spaces before an opening quote and text after a closing quote (trimmed) are tolerated. An unclosed quote drops its record. The header check compares fields[0..1] only, as the plan says. | This is the simplest behaviour that meets every test in the plan. It gives the preview one clear message per line. A file containing only an unclosed quote shows that error instead of 'empty file'. (S1) |

A server-side oracle that does not depend on client code is also a stronger check of the server data. (S5) |
| 2026-11-01 | S5.4 deadlock-free | Five rounds of `save([a,b])` and `save([b,a])` started together. Each round expects no 40P01 and only AppErrors, exactly one winner, and the loser's VERSION_CONFLICT. The loser then replays with the reported versions and completes; versions end at +2. A separate lock-order test holds `hi`, calls `lockAssignments([hi, lo])`, and confirms with a NOWAIT probe (55P03) that `lo` is already locked. | Two batches with the same `baseVersion` cannot both succeed, so 'both complete' means win, or conflict then retry. The NOWAIT probe checks the sorted lock order directly instead of hoping for a rare deadlock. (S5) |
| 2026-11-01 | S5.4 waiting | Race tests wait on an observed lock wait in `pg_stat_activity` (polled, capped at 10 s), never on a fixed sleep. If the watched operation settles early, its own error is thrown at once. | Deterministic, and against the stubs they failed fast with the real NOT_IMPLEMENTED cause. (S5) |

| 2026-11-01 | M7-D4 · BR-24, r5 §3.5 | The client opens the BR-24 dialog **pre-emptively** when its cache shows a DAY week under a WEEK change; the server's `NEEDS_CHOICE` is the authority and opens the same dialog as a fallback. While open, the typed value shows as pending (`data-pending-choice`). Choice → those changes are enqueued with that `onDayWeekConflict`; Cancel → only the conflicting pairs are dropped and reverted, the rest is sent. The save queue never merges changes with different `onDayWeekConflict` values (it flushes first). Undo/redo/collapse always send DAY snapshots or `COLLAPSE` and never open the dialog. | One choice per gesture; no surprise data loss. |

| 2026-11-02 | M8-D6 · BR-71, FR-706, X66 | A bound task is late iff it is **not completed** and any of: a WEEK allocation with `week_start > m.date`, a DAY allocation with `period_start > m.date`, `due_date > m.date`. Plans counted = active assignments of the task **and of its live, not-completed descendants** (a bound parent warns when its subtree is planned after the date; P4). SQL bound: `a.week_start between m.date − 6 and m.date + 1096` and the comparison above (WEEK in the milestone's own week never late; DAY later in the same week is late). Milestone `late` = ≥ 1 late bound task, reported as `lateTaskCount`; computed whatever the milestone's own status (literal BR-71). **Reviewed (Q1): kept** — parents carry no assignments (BR-31), so without the subtree a bound parent could never warn about plans although FR-703 lets users bind parents; the subtree's plans *are* the parent's plans. | Literal BR-71 + X66; P4. |

| 2026-11-02 | M7.1-D30 · FR-813, §8.13 (owner feedback 261102_02 "week misalignment"; supersedes the M7.1-D2 MUI X chart and the M7.1-D5 4–26 clamp/debounce) | The กราฟภาระ chart and its ดูเป็นตาราง table are laid out on the grid's own column geometry: `VirtualGrid` gets `renderBelow(g)` and passes its rendered columns (`vCols`, x = the virtualizer's `start` = frozen width + offset), content width, frozen / sticky "รวม" widths, clientWidth and `follow`, a ref callback whose element's `scrollLeft` it keeps equal to the grid's both ways in the scroll event (scrollbar, wheel, keyboard, วันนี้, the open-column hold; scrolling the chart scrolls the grid). Pure mapping in `src/lib/grid/chart-geometry.ts` (`periodSlots`, `slotWeeks`, `slotAt`, `valueSlotOf`, `niceScale`, `linePath`). The chart is a **hand-written SVG layer** (no `@mui/x-charts`): one chart column per rendered grid column with the same left/width (right border = the grid's column edge, month-start line on the grid's month starts, marker lines at the grid's marker edges), each weekly point and x tick centred on the week's value column — a collapsed week, or the "รวม" column of an expanded week (day columns are chart columns without a point; FR-813 stays weekly); the frozen area holds the y axis, the sticky "รวม" area is a blank cover (the chart plots weekly loads only). x = exactly the weeks whose value column the grid renders (overscan included), no 4–26 clamp, no debounce. The table is an ARIA table on the same slots (headers for every rendered column, value cells in value columns, compact visible text + full text for AT). Proven by `e2e/m7.1-alignment.spec.ts` (≤ 2 px at 1280/1920: open, grid scroll, วันนี้, chart scroll, resized task column, hidden assignees, expanded week). `@mui/x-charts` stays in package.json unused (no uninstall in a fix; remove in a dependency sweep). | MUI X's point/band scale spreads the visible weeks over its own width with its own margins and cannot map mixed-width (64/44/52 px), virtualized, horizontally scrolled columns pixel-exactly; deriving x from the virtualizer itself makes misalignment impossible by construction. |

| 2026-11-02 | M11-D4 · §6.2, CLAUDE.md DB | **One generated migration `<n>_m11_databases`** (schema.ts → `pnpm db:generate --name=m11_databases`): enums `db_column_type`, `db_view_type`; `ALTER TYPE page_kind ADD VALUE 'DB_ROW'` (drizzle-kit emits it from the enum change); tables per SPEC DDL; `pages.database_row_id uuid unique references database_rows(id) on delete cascade`; check **`pages_kind_row`: `(kind::text = 'DB_ROW') = (database_row_id is not null)`** — the `::text` cast is mandatory: PostgreSQL forbids using an enum value added by `ADD VALUE` in the same transaction (55P04 "unsafe use of new value"), and the Drizzle migrator applies every pending migration in **one** transaction; no M11 SQL may use `'DB_ROW'` as an enum literal. Extra indexes beyond SPEC (named explicitly): `database_rows_batch_idx`, `database_columns_batch_idx`, `database_views_batch_idx` (`(deleted_batch) where deleted_batch is not null`) for `restore*Batch`. If drizzle-kit emits a drop/recreate of the enum instead of `ADD VALUE`, move `ALTER TYPE` into a custom migration generated **before** the schema one (still the `::text` rule). `mig` test applies the **whole journal in one transaction** on a scratch DB. | Generated SQL only; the one-transaction migrator would otherwise break fresh installs. |

| 2026-11-02 | BR-91 · roundDecimalString | Rounding is half-up away from zero on the digits, done in BigInt: `-0.5 → -1` at 0 decimals and `9.995 → 10.00`. (S1) |

| 2026-11-03 | M11 close-out · T3 / FR-685 | The e2e check "Cmd/Ctrl+click on ↗ opens a new tab" (`e2e/m6.1-task-page.spec.ts` T3) watches the browser's target list through CDP (`e2e/support/new-tab.ts` `newTabOpenedBy`: a new `page` target in the same browser context whose URL becomes `/t/<id>`, then closed) instead of `context.waitForEvent('page')`. The assertion is unchanged: the click opens a new tab on the task page and the list stays put. A plain click makes the helper fail ("no new tab matching …"), so it still tests what it should. | Root cause, traced with in-page event logging, CDP target events and `DEBUG=pw:protocol`: the click always reached the real `<a>` (Next Link left the modified click alone, `defaultPrevented` false), and Chromium always opened the tab (`Page.frameRequestedNavigation` disposition newTab, a new target titled "งาน · Team Working OS" at `/t/<id>` that fired `load`). Playwright reports a new page only after `Page.frameNavigated` for its first real navigation (`_firstNonInitialNavigationCommittedPromise`). When the opener's renderer (shared by the same-site tab) is busy, which happens right after the Esc → back re-render and more often under full-suite load, Chromium sometimes commits the new tab's document without ever sending `Page.frameNavigated` on its session (`getFrameTree` shows the initial empty frame, then `frameStartedNavigating` → `domContentEventFired` → `load` with no `frameNavigated`). So `waitForEvent('page')` hangs although the product works. Before: 1–5 failures in every 20–25 isolated repeats. After: 20/20 isolated and 3/3 in an m6.1+m10+m11 batch run alongside `pnpm check`. No product change. Not fixed with retries or a longer timeout. |
| 2026-11-03 | M11 close-out · test stability | Two UI tests that failed only under full-suite load now wait for the real condition. `AccountForms.test.tsx` U-ACCF-03 waits with `findByRole` for the idle submit button. The INVALID_PASSWORD error can render while the first submit's transition is still pending, and the button then reads `th.common.saving`. `TeamCalendar.test.tsx` imports `./LeaveDialog` in `beforeAll`, so the first test that opens the React.lazy dialog no longer races a cold dynamic import against `findByRole`'s 1 s timeout. | `pnpm check` failed twice on these two tests during the close-out (each file passed 3/3 alone). No timeout was raised and no product code changed. |

| 2026-11-03 | v3.6 · FR-811/812, BR-103a (new), AC-814, SPEC D29 — owner request | **Project calendar bars from the plan.** A task with neither start nor due date takes its span from its own live assignees' counted (BR-50) allocations: WEEK row → first/last working day of that week per the assignee's `work_schedules` (fraction > 0; no rows → Mon–Fri; holidays/leave ignored like BR-41 `standard`), DAY row → its date; one bar per task over all assignees (pure `src/domain/plan-span.ts` `planDaySpan`). **Partial dates:** the missing side is filled from the plan when the plan reaches past the explicit date (only start S → end = planEnd if planEnd > S; only due D → start = planStart if planStart < D), otherwise the old one-day bar; both dates → explicit only (`barSpan` in `src/lib/plan-calendar/bars.ts`). Parents use only their own assignees (FR-812 "not from children" kept). Bars with any plan side: `data-from-plan` → hatched lighter fill + dashed edge + visible "จากแผน" tag; tooltip second line + accessible name give the source ("วันที่/วันเริ่ม/วันสิ้นสุดจากแผนงาน") and range; click/↗ unchanged; agenda rows the same. **Data:** new `GET /api/projects/[id]/plan-spans?from&to` (month grid, calendarQuerySchema; authz.requireProject view → same visibility as the task list, ids only) — `repo.allocations.plannedWeekBoundsForProject` (one grouped min/max week per assignment, week_start bounded to the grid ± 1096 days = X66) + `listForWeeks` of only the edge weeks + `listSchedules`. Key `qk.projectPlanSpans(projectId, from, to)` under ['workload'] (refreshed by every grid save / invalidateLoad) and `qk.projectPlanSpansAll` in invalidateTasks; the calendar waits for it (an error falls back to explicit dates). Golden: POS R1 31/8–2/10, UAT 14/9–9/10, Rollout 30/11–1/1/2027; Store dashboard 2/11–18/12, requirement 9/11–4/12. Tests: unit `plan-span.test.ts`, `bars.test.ts` › barSpan (+ fast-check), `invalidate.test.ts`; ui `PlanCalendar.test.tsx`, `PlanAgenda.test.tsx`; int `plan-spans.int.test.ts`; authz T-PLANSPANS*; e2e `m11.3-calendar-plan.spec.ts` › CP1 + `m7.1-grid.spec.ts` › P7 (now ไม่มีวันที่ (0)) | Owner: "the plan already says the task starts 2/11 and ends 14/12 — why do I have to enter dates again?" · A separate endpoint, not the task-list payload: that payload is shared by list/board/panel, has no month range and is not refreshed by person-grid saves; filling only the missing side keeps one symmetric rule that never overrides a date the user set; reading only edge weeks keeps the bounded query cheap |

| 2026-11-03 | v3.7 · FR-1417 (new), FR-1442, BR-90/92/93/94/95/97, AC-1415, SPEC D31 — owner request | **Per-cell date ranges.** DATE cells keep a plain `'YYYY-MM-DD'` for single dates (no migration) and store ranges as `{ start, end }` (exactly these two keys, both real ISO dates, `start ≤ end`; zod accepts the object shape, `normalizeCellInput` checks it → `VALIDATION`). Editor: `DateEditor` in `CellEditor.tsx` — start input, "วันสิ้นสุด" switch (MUI `Switch`, role switch) adds an end input, Tab start → end inside a range (elsewhere Tab confirms as before), the calendar picks start then end, end < start → "วันสิ้นสุดต้องไม่ก่อนวันเริ่ม"; typing "3/11 → 7/11" (also `->`, `–`, `—`, ` - `) gives a range without the switch. Text form `2026-11-03 → 2026-11-07` (BR-93, round-trips). Sort key = start then end (a single date is a one-day span). Filters: is/before/after/on-or-… compare the start; new op `within` ("อยู่ในช่วง", value `{ kind: 'range', start, end }`) = overlap. Display `formatThaiDateSpan`: "3–7 พ.ย. 2569", "30 พ.ย. – 4 ธ.ค. 2569", cross-year both years. Calendar: `calendarMonth` returns `ranges` + per-day `rangeRows`; bars laid out by the plan calendar's `monthBars` (≤ 3 lanes per week, overflow in "+N"); pointer drag on the bar moves keeping the length (grab day → day under the pointer via `elementsFromPoint`, 4 px slop), drag on a 6 px edge handle resizes, `Alt+←/→` moves a day, `Alt+Shift+←/→` changes the end, "ย้ายไปวันที่…" on a range row keeps its length; any range value (even one day) is a bar, single dates stay cards. | Simplest shape that keeps old data valid and is unambiguous at the server; reuses the tested lane layout of the project calendar instead of a second one. |
| 2026-11-03 | v3.8 · FR-668/669, §7.8, SPEC D32 — owner request | **Team tasks ตาราง + ปฏิทิน (M11.4).** One new read route `GET /api/tasks/plan-spans?from&to[&projectId]` (not N per-project calls): same visibility as `/api/tasks` in SQL, ids + dates only. `tasks.view` enum extended (project tab reads TABLE/CALENDAR as LIST); `?view=` wins over the preference. Table/calendar fetch every page (cap 2,000, then a hint to filter) because sorting and month placement need the whole set. | One authz point and one request per month; reuses the project calendar and existing actions — no schema or action changes. |
| 2026-11-03 | v3.8 · FR-668 ภาระรวม | **Load column in days, own assignees only.** `plannedCd` = Σ counted (BR-50) planned load of the task's own live assignments in today ± 3 years (X66 horizon), integer cd, shown as วัน (not %) — a task has no capacity to divide by; parents show their own (usually none). | Cheap one grouped query per page; % would need an arbitrary denominator. |
| 2026-11-03 | v3.8 · FR-668 edits | **Edits reuse existing actions and panel gating.** Status → `useSetTaskStatus`; assignees → M3 `AssigneePicker`/`AssigneeChips` on the task's project data (members, LEAD/Manager reach, leaf-only) with an optimistic mirror on the /tasks cache (`onAssigned`); dates → `updateTask` (one-click DateCalendar, Delete clears, undo entry); milestone → `useMilestoneMutations.link` (task-edit right, as in the panel — not LEAD-only, matching the server rule); delete → `deleteTaskTree` + undo toast. Archived projects are not listed on /tasks, so every row is editable for its viewer. | No new authz surface; behaviour identical to the task panel. |
| 2026-11-03 | v3.8 · FR-669 | **Team calendar draws no holidays, leave or milestones**; bars coloured per project with a dot, project name in tooltip/accessible name; the "แสดงงานที่เสร็จแล้ว" filter decides done tasks (no separate calendar switch). | Owner: keep simple; colour never alone. |

## Deviations

_(จุดที่ทำต่างจาก spec เพราะข้อจำกัดทางเทคนิค)_

| วันที่ | อ้างอิง | spec บอกว่า | ทำจริง | เหตุผล |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-09-30 | §7.1, §7.2, §12 M0 | Tailwind CSS v4 + shadcn/ui; `@blocknote/shadcn`; cmdk มากับ shadcn | Material UI 9 (+ CSS Modules สำหรับเซลล์กริด); `@blocknote/mantine` ปรับธีมให้เข้ากับ MUI; cmdk แบบ headless ใน MUI Dialog | เจ้าของผลิตภัณฑ์เลือก MUI (tech-stack.md); BlockNote ไม่มี package สำหรับ MUI — แก้ SPEC §7.1/§7.2/§12 แล้ว |
| 2026-10-30 | §8.7 | --load-light-fg / --tentative-fg = #9C6500 | #8F5C00; added --surface, --faded-fg; dark set defined | #9C6500 on #FFEB9C = 4.12:1 < 4.5:1 required by §8.9 (X51, M0-D22) |
| 2026-10-30 | §7.2 | drizzle/9999_triggers.sql | journaled custom migrations (drizzle-kit generate --custom) | the migrator runs only journaled files (M0-D25) |
| 2026-10-30 | §6.2 (BA tables "generate ด้วย CLI") | Better Auth tables generated by the Better Auth CLI | Tables hand-written in `src/server/db/schema.ts` (timestamptz, uuid FK, `role` default, app-owned `deactivated_on`); the CLI is used only for `pnpm auth:check` (`auth check schema`), which passes | The generated schema uses text ids/timestamps that do not match our conventions; drizzle-kit owns migrations (M1-D1) |
| 2026-10-30 | BR-02 | convert with `Math.round(parseFloat(v) * 100)` | `numericToCd` parses the decimal string (`/^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$/`), no float; same result for every `numeric(≤4,2)` value | CLAUDE.md bans float arithmetic on load (BR-01, M1-D22); U-UNIT-06 guards it |
| 2026-11-01 | §7.1, §7.2 (M4-D1) | `@blocknote/shadcn` | `@blocknote/mantine` 0.55.0 + `@mantine/core`/`@mantine/hooks` 9.6.3, themed to MUI through `--bn-*` variables, Mantine CSS scoped to `.bn-mantine` | Same owner decision as the first row (OWN-2): BlockNote has no MUI package; not a new deviation, recorded for M4 |
| 2026-11-01 | §7.3, CLAUDE.md "เขียนผ่าน server action" (M4-D2, X63) | Every write goes through a server action | The leave-page save (FR-531) is a route-handler write `PUT /api/pages/[id]/content` (`keepalive`, body ≤ 60 KB from the client, same `requirePage(edit)` → `savePageContent` → audit as the action, Origin check M4-D18); every other save uses the `savePageContent` server action | A server action cannot run from `pagehide`, and keepalive bodies are capped at 64 KiB (R14) |
| 2026-11-01 | §6.2 (M4-D25) | Cover FK named `pages_cover_fk` | Drizzle's generated name `pages_cover_file_id_files_id_fk`, plus one extra index `files_project_id_idx` | Migrations are never hand-edited; behaviour is identical |
| 2026-11-01 | BR-25, BR-26 (M6-D6) | Boundary weeks get DAY rows by distributing N over the in-range days by `dayCapacity` | Distribute by `dayCapacity`, then clamp-and-spill within the in-range days (and within the out-of-range part of the week); reject with `VALIDATION` `UNREPRESENTABLE` when a day would still exceed 100 cd or a WEEK row 700 cd | BR-26 and the DDL check `person_days <= 1` forbid more than 1.00 per day (golden G3: Ivy 4 days over Wed–Fri); details in Decisions › M6-D6 |
| 2026-11-01 | §8.12 (M6.1-D21) | `--text-secondary #6B7280` | `#646A75` light / `#A3A9B4` dark | `#6B7280` on `--hover-bg #F3F4F6` is 4.39:1 < 4.5:1 required by §8.9 (like X51); the closest grey that passes on surface, hover-bg and surface-muted |
| 2026-11-01 | M7-D7 · BR-22, BR-24, BR-26 | DAY edits/SCALE store BR-22/BR-24 values as computed | DAY values clamp at 100 cd and the excess spills to non-fixed days (X65 tiers); nothing to absorb → `VALIDATION`/`UNREPRESENTABLE`. SCALE spills too. The save service enforces it. | SPEC-internal conflict BR-22/BR-24 vs BR-26 + DDL `person_days <= 1` (see Decisions M7-D7). |

## Known issues / TODO ถัดไป

- **v3.7 (2026-11-03) — date ranges, nested databases, editor fixes; first e2e run of 8a51242…247c34b:** `pnpm check` green (479 files, 8,897 passed, 16 skipped); full `pnpm test:e2e` 264 passed / 2 skipped. Failures found on the way and their root causes: (1) row right-click / Shift+F10 did nothing in a real page — `inTextField` treated the editor root (`[contenteditable=true][role=textbox]`) around the block as a text field (fixed: stop at the `contenteditable=false` node view); (2) the new column's menu closed at once — `createColumn` answers its temp id before the optimistic column reaches the cache (fixed: close only after the column was seen); (3) m11 D8 saved "ยบายเบิก" — rows keyed by id re-mounted on temp → server id and the open title editor lost the typed characters (fixed: `stableDbKeys`); (4) range-bar drag: pointerdown now prevents the compatibility mousedown so the editor never starts a native drag mid-gesture; test-only: D2b (create item rightly offered for a partial name), D2c (modal menu makes the page aria-hidden; MUI shifts a tall menu up to the viewport), D2d (⤢ is display:none until hover), D5b (tall window so the dragged-to days are on screen), m10 P6 (typed before the editor was editable), m4 E6 (ivy leaves by navigation — `page.close()` could drop the keepalive lease DELETE). D15 (nested databases, incl. the synthetic paste of a database block at level 3) passed as written.
  - Follow-up D done without code: `/r/<outer row>?db=<nested db>&row=<row>` already opens the nested row's panel (the row body's database block runs as `variant="page"`); D15 now asserts it.
  - Date ranges: the "วันสิ้นสุด" switch itself is mouse/touch only — keyboard users type a range ("3/11 → 7/11") or Tab from start to end once a stored range is open; calendar bars move by Alt+←/→ and change their end by Alt+Shift+←/→ (no keyboard way to move only the start).
  - Lease release still relies on a `pagehide` keepalive DELETE; a browser that drops it leaves the lease until its TTL (behaviour unchanged, only the test now leaves by navigation).


- **M11.2 (2026-11-03):**
  - /workload group-header rows (`th[scope=rowgroup]`, colspan = whole table) scroll away horizontally: their `position: sticky; left: 0` cannot move a cell as wide as the table (pre-existing since M9; visible with long windows). The month lines also skip those rows by design.
  - The chart table ("ดูเป็นตาราง") reuses the grid's `periodColIndex` (2 frozen ARIA columns) — on /workload its `aria-colindex` skips index 2 (one frozen column); valid ARIA, left as is.

- **M11.1 (2026-11-03):**
  - The owner's preview server on port 3300 (`plan-system-preview`, a separate checkout running M11 code) shares the dev DB, which `pnpm db:migrate` moved to 0017: its plan-marker reads (GET plan-markers, the ⚑ menu) now fail until that checkout is updated to m11.1. Not touched.
  - Machine load: with the Docker VM busy (many unrelated containers), full `pnpm test` runs showed 17–26 random timeouts / deadlocks in unrelated suites (matrix, editor, settings); every failing file passed in isolation and the next full `pnpm check` on an idle DB (old `planner_t_*` scratch DBs dropped) was green. e2e `m4-pages E6` (lease hand-back keepalive) and `m4 E16` / `m7.1 P7` failed once each under load and passed on re-run.
  - Home "ภาระของฉัน" card keeps days (M11.1-D4); the project grid footer keeps days + its "% ใช้กำลังคน" row (only the person grid footer follows `load.display`).
  - `@mui/x-charts` is still in package.json and unused since M7.1-D30 (dependency sweep in M12).

- **M11 process note (2026-11-03):** stream S2 (backend) died on a network error part-way through. The integration agent finished its work (remaining §7.9 actions, row bodies, search) before G2, and the S6 authz/mask/race suites and G2–G4 cover it. The first close-out was blocked by the flaky T3 Cmd+click check; see the 2026-11-03 T3 Decision row.
- **E2E stability (2026-11-03):** an m6.1+m10+m11 `--repeat-each=3` batch, run while `pnpm check` was using the CPU, also failed on timing in m10 B1/B6/P5, m11 D8 (locked wiki, title autosave poll 5 s), and m6.1 T2/T7/V4. All of them passed in the full `pnpm test:e2e` run used for this close-out (247 passed, 2 skipped, with no other load). They show up only under heavy parallel load; check again in M12 if they appear in a normal full run.

- **M11 close-out (2026-11-03), stream notes (S0–S6 reports; most pre-integration items were resolved at G2 — kept for the record):**
  - S0: G0: `pnpm check` exits 1 only on tests/authz/matrix.int.test.ts › T-META-01 (19 actions without rows) and tests/authz/coverage.int.test.ts › AZ-2 (2 routes without rows), until S6 registers the rows. Typecheck and lint are green, as is every other test (the full run had 422 of 425 files passing; the third failure, th.test, was fixed afterwards and re-run green).
  - S0: KaTeX lazy loading is checked only against build output (HTML and chunk graph, served assets, CSP header), not a browser network log. e2e P2 should confirm that pages without equations request nothing containing 'katex' or 'KaTeX_'.
  - S0: Running prettier on files I own (actions/pages.ts, world.ts, world.int.test.ts, schema.int.test.ts, th.ts and others) also re-wrapped some existing lines in those files. The code is unchanged; it only adds diff noise.
  - S0: fakeDbApi converts column types in a simplified way: values that do not fit the new type are dropped. The real BR-93 rules are S1's convertColumn.
  - S0: Under the S0 stubs, opening a DB_ROW page id through /p or any page API returns 404 until S2 adds the authz branch.
  - S1: Fixture data: Sorting and grouping by person rely on the ICU Thai collator placing names with a leading vowel (ไอวี่) under their consonant: กมล < ซินดี้ < มินท์ < ไอวี่. This holds on the current Node, but a Node built without full ICU would order these names differently.
  - S1: Calendar test vectors: The plan's step S1.10 mentions 'Feb 2027' and 'a 4-week February' as two cases. They are the same month: 2027-02-01 is a Monday and 2027-02-28 a Sunday. I also tested Feb 2026 (5 weeks) and Dec 2026 (5 weeks, year rollover).
  - S6: There are 472 matrix role-cases, 8 masking tests, 1 golden test and 6 race tests that fail before integration. All fail on S2's NOT_IMPLEMENTED stubs, on S0's temporary requirePage DB_ROW branch that returns 404 for everyone (RB-PAGE-GET, RB-SAVE*, RB-LOCK*, RB-UPLOAD, RB-KEEPALIVE), or because `databases` is missing from /api/search. I found no real product defect, since none of this code is implemented yet. Rerun after S2 with: pnpm vitest run --project integration tests/authz tests/integration tests/golden.
  - S6: The full matrix now has about 940 more role-cases that each build the 'databases' world. The M11 subset alone took about 230 s locally, so CI time grows accordingly.
  - S6: The `tsc` errors still in the tree come from S1 (src/domain/database*.test.ts: './database' missing) and S5 (inline-equation.test.tsx). None come from tests/**.
  - S5: Not verified in a real browser (e2e D10, D11, E1–E4, P2): a copy/paste round trip that carries the custom clipboard type, the side menu deleting the database block, and no KaTeX request on pages without equations. Unit tests cover the logic only.
  - S5: If a browser strips the custom clipboard type when pasting into another tab, the paste falls back to 'ไม่พบฐานข้อมูล'. This is the planned known issue (plan §1.2).
  - S5: Not covered: the clipboard and internal copy of a database block render the block's `render` (the whole DatabaseBlockView) through BlockNote's elementRenderer, as M10's live blocks already do. This works but is heavy for large databases; worth a check in the P2 perf smoke.
  - S5: Not covered: Enter or Space on a focused block equation opens the popover; whether ProseMirror also acts on that key (the equation block stays selectable so Backspace can delete it) needs checking in e2e E1.
  - S5: No BlockNote limitation forced a Deviation.
  - S3: Nothing was checked in a real browser (no Playwright in this stream): dnd-kit row, column, tab and option drags; resize by pointer; virtualisation and sticky columns with real layout; where the Drawer sits under the app header; dark-mode looks; and the 390 px phone layout. e2e D1–D7, D14 and V1–V3 at G2 cover these.
  - S3: S2's real actions were mocked in every test. The data layer assumes S2's actions return the DTOs in src/lib/contracts/databases.ts, and that `VERSION_CONFLICT` carries the object DTO as `current` (read through `ActionFailure.extra.current`).
  - S3: KANBAN and CALENDAR tabs render "—" until S4's modules are imported in view-modules.ts (see requests).
  - S3: The panel's compact row body uses S5's RowBody and the GET /api/database-rows/[id]/body route (S2); tests mock RowBody.
  - S3: An undo entry whose object later had a conflict does nothing and shows nothing (M11-D14 "dropped"); the conflict toast is the only feedback the user gets.
  - S3: Row virtualisation uses a fixed estimate (34 px rows, 36 px below 600 px) and does not measure rows; very long wrapped content cannot occur because table cells are one line.
  - S4: The kanban and calendar tabs show '—' in the real block until S3 adds the two register imports to view-modules.ts.
  - S4: Drag moves have Cmd+Z undo but no 'เลิกทำ' toast until updateRow can take a toast option. This affects AC-1405, which clicks 'เลิกทำ' after a calendar drag; see requests.
  - S4: /r/[rowId] and the /p → /r redirect depend on S2's getRowPageView and getPageView rowId; services.rowPages.getRowPageView was still a NOT_IMPLEMENTED stub when I read it.
  - S4: Calendar drag uses dnd-kit with pointerWithin, or rectIntersection for the keyboard. Its drop logic (dropValue) and the date-picker path are unit-tested, but a keyboard drag in the calendar is only proven in the kanban test; e2e D5 should cover a real pointer drag.
  - S4: The calendar grid uses role=grid / row / gridcell with tabbable buttons inside the cells. There is no roving arrow-key focus between days; keyboard users reach every action by Tab and the ⋯ menus.
  - Plan §10 known issues: cross-tab paste without the custom clipboard type shows "ไม่พบฐานข้อมูล" (M11-D13); detached databases are kept forever until the Phase-2 trash; phone is read + basic edit only (M11-D31).
  - M12 forward notes (plan/m11 §10): perf gates NFR-11/12 (P-DB1, PF-DB1…4), `seed:perf` via `addSyntheticDatabase` (2,000 databases / 200,000 rows), audit sweep incl. entity `database` and row-body page saves, offline allowlist for KaTeX MathML namespace + fonts in the image, README third-party notice for `katex` (MIT).

- **M6.1 (2026-11-01):** project / client **edit** dialogs are restyled (light inputs) but not converted to light forms (M6.1-D23) — owner to confirm. The M6.1-D27 `pg_dump` before 0011 could not be taken at the integration gate: the dev DB already had migrations 0009–0011 applied during the build (it then held 13 tasks and no TASK pages, i.e. test data, no owner notes visible); production upgrades must still back up first.

- **M6 integration (2026-11-01):** `m4-pages` E16 (Cmd+K over an editor text selection keeps the palette closed) failed once in a full e2e run and passed in the next full run and in 3 repeats; it touches no M6 code (palette/BlockNote selection timing). Watch it at the next gates. The G1 chunk assertion uses `from=2027-04-12` (see Decisions). Fixed at integration: empty grid cells had height 0 (not clickable) — `.cell`/`.footerCell` now span the row; task mutations now invalidate the project's grid (`invalidateTasks`), so rows created/renamed/moved elsewhere appear without a reload; the FR-612 dialog ignores the partial dates the picker emits while a year is typed.

- pnpm 12 rejects short flags such as `-s`/`--silent` before the command (`pnpm -s check` exits 2 with 'unexpected argument'). Agents run `pnpm check` and redirect its output instead.
- **แผน implement ครบแล้ว (2026-10-30):** `PLAN.md` (X01–X96) + `plan/m00…m11-*.md` ทั้ง 12 ไฟล์ (เขียน + review + ตรวจความสอดคล้องข้ามไฟล์, ผ่าน structure check) + `plan/traceability.md` — (ณ ตอนนั้นยังไม่มีโค้ดและยังไม่ได้ `git init` — M0 เสร็จแล้ว 2026-10-30)
- SPEC v3.1 (2026-10-30): สถานะงานต่อโปรเจค, ตัวขวาง, บอร์ด, `/tasks`, บล็อกข้อมูลสดในหน้า — แนวคิดยืมจาก AFFiNE แต่ไม่ใช้โค้ด AFFiNE (PLAN §1.7); การตัดสินใจทั้งหมดอยู่ใน PLAN §1.4 X70–X96
- จุดที่ต้องพิสูจน์ตอน implement (ยังไม่ได้ verify): cmdk group accessible name (X88), event isolation ของบล็อกใน BlockNote 0.55 (M10 S3.1 spike), โฟลเดอร์ `api/search/index` ใน App Router (M10 S0.13), deploy facts ใน X94
- โฟลเดอร์ `affine/` (AFFiNE self-host ทดลอง, มี `data/` ของ Postgres) อยู่ใน repo นี้ — ตอน M0 `git init` ใส่ `affine/` ใน `.gitignore` (m00 S0.8 ทำแล้ว) และ `.dockerignore` (M11)
- **M0 close-out (2026-10-30):** ESLint 9 is EOL since 2026-08-06; upgrading to ESLint 10 is its own Decision (M0-D1). Node 22 reaches EOL on 2027-04-30. A strict style CSP (no `'unsafe-inline'` for styles) is deferred to M11 (optional). Date pickers are not installed yet; the first milestone that renders a `DatePicker` adds them (M0-D16).
- M0 · `/home` shows the real request-time date in Asia/Bangkok until M1 switches it to `formatThaiDate(serverToday())` (M0-D6); the M0 e2e does not assert a fixed date. (S2)
- M0 · Every Vitest run prints a harmless Vite 8 warning: 'ESM syntax in a file loaded as CommonJS (vitest.config.ts)'; the future `configLoader: 'native'` default will not support it. It does not fail the run. Fix = rename to `vitest.config.mts`; adding `"type": "module"` instead would break `__dirname` in `next.config.ts` and the CommonJS script assumption (risk 23). Left as is; a later Decision. (S0, S2)
- M0 · The domain coverage threshold (95%) cannot be proven to block yet because no `src/domain` files exist (M1 adds them). Lint guards were proven with transient probe files, then deleted: Thai literal, `localStorage`, `window.sessionStorage`, `@ts-ignore`, `any`, `eslint-disable` without a reason, React imported into domain, and `new Date` / `Date.now` in domain all report errors. (S0)
- M0 · `next typegen` (part of `pnpm typecheck`) creates `.next/types` (gitignored). Streams should use `pnpm exec tsc --noEmit` while others are mid-flight (risk 6). (S0)
- M0 · Resolved at integration: S1 reported `pnpm check` exiting 2 mid-build because other streams' files (`e2e/m0-smoke.spec.ts` importing `src/lib/format`, `EmptyState.test.tsx`) were unfinished. At close-out `pnpm check` is green. (S1)
- M0 · e2e test 2 computes the expected `/home` date from the real current time (`formatThaiInstantDate(new Date())`, M0-D6); a run that crosses Bangkok midnight between render and assertion could fail. M1 removes this with the server clock and `APP_NOW`. (S3)
- M0 · e2e test 6 relies on Chromium moving focus to `<main id=main tabIndex=-1>` when the skip-link fragment is followed, and test 9 allows only console errors matching `/status of 404/`. Both passed at close-out; if test 9 breaks later, narrow the pattern, never widen it. (S3)
- M0 · Before `pnpm test:e2e`, make sure nothing else listens on port 3100: `reuseExistingServer: true` would silently reuse a stale server (e.g. a dev build), and the CSP test would fail on `unsafe-eval`. (S3)
- M0 → M1 forward note (m00 §7.3): tests 1–7 and 9 need `loginAs(page, 'manager')`, and test 2's date check becomes `th.home.today(formatThaiDate('2026-09-30'))`. (S3)

- **M1 close-out (2026-10-30), still open:**
  - AC-203 is done only for M1 (login blocked, sessions revoked, `listAssignableUsers()` excludes deactivated users, "ปิดใช้งาน" chip). The picker clauses come in M2/M3 and the grid label in M6 (X12). **Closed 2026-11-01:** the grid clause shipped in M6 (e2e G11 — "(ปิดใช้งาน)" on the plan row, still editable).
  - FR-106: lockout writes happen outside the sign-in transaction, so parallel bursts can overshoot the 5-attempt limit by a few attempts (R-M1-18).

The rows check content and exact shapes, so a regression will still fail them.
